# Test source

```ts
  180 | 
  181 |   /**
  182 |    * ATC: Open a Story whose chain is exactly 1 layer deep at every step
  183 |    * through Run (1 AC -> 1 ATC -> 1 Test -> 1 Run) - expects the single-row
  184 |    * case to render exactly, not as a zero-row false-empty state and not with
  185 |    * any duplicated row (BK-45 AC-01, BVA-lower-boundary partition / TC-BK45-02
  186 |    * / BK-456).
  187 |    *
  188 |    * Deliberately narrower than `expectChainRenders` (@atc('BK-445')): no
  189 |    * Defect-layer assertion (out of this TC's stated Given/Then scope), and
  190 |    * exact-count checks (`toHaveCount(1)`) rather than `>= 1` checks - the
  191 |    * exactness IS the boundary being tested, not an incidental side effect of
  192 |    * happy-path coverage.
  193 |    *
  194 |    * Fixed assertions:
  195 |    *  - chain-view root visible
  196 |    *  - exactly one AC card renders (not zero, not more than one)
  197 |    *  - that AC card shows a non-empty title
  198 |    *  - exactly one ATC row renders inside it (not zero, not more than one)
  199 |    *  - that ATC row shows a non-empty title, layer badge, linked Test name,
  200 |    *    and Run status pill
  201 |    *
  202 |    * @param args - the Project slug and the Story to open
  203 |    * @param args.projectSlug - Project slug the Story belongs to
  204 |    * @param args.userStoryId - Story to open the chain for (must already
  205 |    *   satisfy the exactly-1-per-layer precondition through Run)
  206 |    */
  207 |   @atc('BK-456')
  208 |   async expectMinimalChainRenders(args: { projectSlug: string, userStoryId: string }): Promise<void> {
  209 |     await this.goto(args);
  210 | 
  211 |     const chainView = this.page.locator('[data-testid="traceability-chain-view"]');
  212 |     await expect(chainView).toBeVisible({ timeout: 15000 });
  213 | 
  214 |     const acCards = chainView.locator('[data-testid^="traceability-ac-"]');
  215 |     await expect(acCards).toHaveCount(1);
  216 | 
  217 |     const acCard = acCards.first();
  218 |     await expect(acCard).not.toBeEmpty();
  219 | 
  220 |     const atcRows = acCard.locator('[data-testid^="traceability-atc-row-"]');
  221 |     await expect(atcRows).toHaveCount(1);
  222 | 
  223 |     const atcRow = atcRows.first();
  224 | 
  225 |     const atcTitle = atcRow.locator('div.truncate.text-fg-1').first();
  226 |     await expect(atcTitle).not.toHaveText('');
  227 | 
  228 |     const atcLayerBadge = atcRow.locator('span.status-chip').first();
  229 |     await expect(atcLayerBadge).not.toHaveText('');
  230 | 
  231 |     const testName = atcRow.locator('span.truncate.text-fg-2').first();
  232 |     await expect(testName).not.toHaveText('');
  233 | 
  234 |     const runStatusPill = atcRow.locator('span.status-chip').nth(1);
  235 |     await expect(runStatusPill).not.toHaveText('');
  236 |   }
  237 | 
  238 |   /**
  239 |    * ATC: Open the route with no `?story=` param - expects the prompt state,
  240 |    * not the chain view and no fetch attempted (BK-45 TC-BK45-23 / BK-453).
  241 |    *
  242 |    * Registers a request collector before navigation (not a `page.route`
  243 |    * mock/abort) so the guard observes rather than blocks — the point is
  244 |    * proving the chain-fetch endpoint was never called, not preventing it.
  245 |    *
  246 |    * @param projectSlug - a valid Project slug
  247 |    */
  248 |   @atc('BK-453')
  249 |   async expectNoStorySelectedPrompt(projectSlug: string): Promise<void> {
  250 |     const chainFetchRequests: string[] = [];
  251 |     this.page.on('request', (request) => {
  252 |       if (/\/v1\/projects\/[^/]+\/traceability(?:\?|$)/.test(request.url())) {
  253 |         chainFetchRequests.push(request.url());
  254 |       }
  255 |     });
  256 | 
  257 |     await this.page.goto(this.buildUrl(`/projects/${projectSlug}/traceability`));
  258 | 
  259 |     await expect(this.page.locator('[data-testid="traceability-no-story-selected"]')).toBeVisible({ timeout: 10000 });
  260 |     await expect(this.page.locator('[data-testid="traceability-chain-view"]')).not.toBeVisible();
  261 |     expect(chainFetchRequests, 'expected no chain-fetch network request when the story param is absent').toHaveLength(0);
  262 |   }
  263 | 
  264 |   /**
  265 |    * ATC: Export the current chain as a self-contained snapshot document -
  266 |    * expects a file download whose suggested filename carries the project
  267 |    * slug and a second-granular export timestamp (BK-50 TC01, D26 pattern).
  268 |    *
  269 |    * Awaits the `download` event concurrently with the click (`Promise.all`)
  270 |    * rather than clicking then polling for a file — the latter is the flaky
  271 |    * pattern under this repo's `retries: 0` policy.
  272 |    *
  273 |    * @param saveTo - filesystem path to save the downloaded document to
  274 |    * @returns the path the snapshot was saved to
  275 |    */
  276 |   @atc('BK-331')
  277 |   async exportSnapshot(saveTo: string): Promise<string> {
  278 |     const [download] = await Promise.all([
  279 |       this.page.waitForEvent('download'),
> 280 |       this.page.locator('[data-testid="traceability-export-button"]').click(),
      |                                                                       ^ Error: locator.click: Error: strict mode violation: locator('[data-testid="traceability-export-button"]') resolved to 2 elements:
  281 |     ]);
  282 | 
  283 |     expect(download.suggestedFilename()).toMatch(/^trace-.+-\d{8}-\d{6}\.html$/);
  284 | 
  285 |     await download.saveAs(saveTo);
  286 | 
  287 |     return saveTo;
  288 |   }
  289 | 
  290 |   /**
  291 |    * ATC: Open the traceability screen with no authenticated session -
  292 |    * expects a redirect to `/login` carrying the original path in `next`,
  293 |    * with no chain markup rendered before the redirect (BK-50 TC04).
  294 |    *
  295 |    * Opens a FRESH browser context with no `storageState` — the shared
  296 |    * `page` from the `ui`/`test` fixtures is already authenticated, so the
  297 |    * anonymous caller needs its own context, not a cleared one.
  298 |    *
  299 |    * @param path - the traceability path to request, e.g. `/projects/{slug}/traceability?story={id}`
  300 |    */
  301 |   @atc('BK-334')
  302 |   async expectAnonymousRedirectToLogin(path: string): Promise<void> {
  303 |     const browser = this.page.context().browser();
  304 |     if (!browser) {
  305 |       throw new Error('expectAnonymousRedirectToLogin requires a browser-backed page context.');
  306 |     }
  307 | 
  308 |     // `storageState: undefined` is required, not cosmetic: the `browser` fixture
  309 |     // here is the "e2e" project's own browser, and that project sets
  310 |     // `use.storageState` to the authenticated qa3 session — a bare
  311 |     // `browser.newContext()` silently INHERITS it, so the "anonymous" caller
  312 |     // was actually still authenticated as qa3 (confirmed via BK-990 follow-up
  313 |     // investigation: `anonymousContext.cookies()` carried the qa3 Supabase
  314 |     // auth cookie). Explicitly clearing it is the only way to get a genuinely
  315 |     // logged-out context from this browser instance.
  316 |     const anonymousContext = await browser.newContext({ storageState: undefined });
  317 |     try {
  318 |       const anonymousPage = await anonymousContext.newPage();
  319 |       await anonymousPage.goto(this.buildUrl(path));
  320 | 
  321 |       await anonymousPage.waitForURL(/\/login(\?|$)/, { timeout: 10000 });
  322 |       const redirectUrl = new URL(anonymousPage.url());
  323 |       expect(redirectUrl.pathname).toBe('/login');
  324 |       expect(redirectUrl.searchParams.get('next')).toBe(path);
  325 | 
  326 |       await expect(anonymousPage.locator('[data-testid="traceability-chain-view"]')).not.toBeVisible();
  327 |     }
  328 |     finally {
  329 |       await anonymousContext.close();
  330 |     }
  331 |   }
  332 | 
  333 |   /**
  334 |    * ATC: Open the traceability route with a target story ID the caller
  335 |    * cannot see (a same-project random UUID standing in for "foreign or
  336 |    * nonexistent" - see the component-level empirical note below for why the
  337 |    * two collapse into the same observable outcome) - expects the shared
  338 |    * app-wide not-found state, never the chain view, and zero chain data
  339 |    * (AC/ATC/Test/Run/Defect) anywhere in the DOM (BK-45 AC-05 / TC-BK45-16,
  340 |    * Decision Table rules 3+4, collapsed - same outcome, deliberate
  341 |    * non-disclosure).
  342 |    *
  343 |    * CORRECTED MECHANISM (empirically confirmed live against staging,
  344 |    * 2026-08-25 - supersedes the component docblock's `traceability-error` /
  345 |    * `traceability-retry` testids and the doctrine's assumed "User story not
  346 |    * found." literal string and network-interceptable 404, none of which
  347 |    * exist on this build for this route):
  348 |    * - The route is server-rendered as a single document navigation that
  349 |    *   always responds HTTP 200, whether the story resolves or not - the
  350 |    *   underlying `GET /v1/projects/{id}/traceability?story={id}` 404
  351 |    *   happens server-side and is never exposed as a client-observable
  352 |    *   network response, so there is nothing for `page.waitForResponse` to
  353 |    *   catch here, retry or not.
  354 |    * - The client renders the shared app-wide `[data-testid="workbench-not-
  355 |    *   found"]` state ("This item is no longer available" / "It may have
  356 |    *   been deleted, or you don't have access to it. The rest of the project
  357 |    *   is still here." / "Back to project") - NOT `traceability-error`, and
  358 |    *   there is no Retry control on this state at all.
  359 |    * - The literal string "User story not found." does not appear anywhere
  360 |    *   in the DOM for this route today (checked via full-page `innerHTML`).
  361 |    *   The non-disclosure property that IS provable at this layer, and is
  362 |    *   this ATC's actual contract, is: the SAME generic not-found state
  363 |    *   renders regardless of *why* the story is unreachable, and zero chain
  364 |    *   data ever leaks into it. The distinct 404 status code + `not_found`
  365 |    *   error code are an API-layer property that this UI test does not assert.
  366 |    *
  367 |    * @param args - the Project slug and the target Story ID to probe
  368 |    * @param args.projectSlug - Project slug to build the route against
  369 |    * @param args.targetStoryId - a foreign-workspace story ID or a random
  370 |    *   nonexistent UUID (caller's responsibility to generate/discover)
  371 |    */
  372 |   @atc('BK-448')
  373 |   async expectNonDisclosureNotFound(args: { projectSlug: string, targetStoryId: string }): Promise<void> {
  374 |     await this.goto({ projectSlug: args.projectSlug, userStoryId: args.targetStoryId });
  375 |

  377 |     await expect(this.page.locator('[data-testid="traceability-chain-view"]')).not.toBeVisible();
  378 | 
  379 |     const acCount = await this.page.locator('[data-testid^="traceability-ac-"]').count();
```
